        ### Task Summary
        Sprint 1 of the Astrid reshape introduces per-tab sessions, a `runs/<ulid>/lease.json` writer-epoch, and — most critically — a flock-protected event-append helper with last-hash + epoch CAS that replaces the current unsafe `append_event` in `astrid/core/task/events.py`. All mutating verbs and inbox consumption must route through that locked path. Sprint 1 also adds `~/.astrid` identity/config/sessions plumbing, a new `astrid attach / sessions / status` CLI surface with a session gate on every other verb, deletes `<project>/active_run.json` and the `astrid threads` subsystem (migrating both into session bindings), and writes a `default_timeline_id: null` sentinel into `project.json` for Sprint 2.

The new helper must do flock(LOCK_EX) → re-read last line and parse hash → CAS against writer's expected_prev_hash → re-read lease.json's writer_epoch → CAS against expected_writer_epoch → append → fsync → release lock.         ### Constraints
        - Sprint 1 must NOT change the step model. No edits to plan.py / inbox.py (beyond switching to the locked append helper and dropping clear_active_run) / step adapter logic.
- Sprint 1 must NOT implement the timeline container — only write a `default_timeline_id: null` sentinel into project.json.
- Sprint 1 must NOT touch RunPod / vibecomfy / hype port / auditing verbs (`run show`, `artifacts`, `trace`) — those are Sprints 5a / 5b.
- The locked-append helper must hold ONE flock(LOCK_EX) across last-hash re-read + epoch re-read + append + fsync. Splitting these is the bug.
- Both CAS failures (stale tail, stale epoch) must raise typed exceptions including the conflicting values in the message — no silent fallbacks anywhere in the lease/append path.
- Takeover must atomically increment writer_epoch in the SAME locked write that swaps attached_session.json. The takeover event is observability; the lock + CAS is the fence.
- All existing Sprint 0 tests must continue to pass (env-inheritance spike, flock-APFS spike, two-tab harness smoke).
- Migration scripts default to --dry-run; --apply commits; idempotent; log per-action to stderr; per-project audit log to temp file. Any data loss in `active_run.json` migration is a STOP-LINE.
- Migration script that adds `default_timeline_id` must keep `project.json` valid against the existing validator (decide additive vs schema-version-bump).
- Astrid is now a SESSION-FIRST CLI: only attach, status, projects ls, projects create, sessions ls, sessions takeover, init, --help are allowed unbound. Every other verb errors with a clear hint.
- Subprocesses must inherit ASTRID_SESSION_ID — confirmed by Sprint 0 env-inheritance spike; do not silently scrub env in any new code path.
- SKILL.md and AGENTS.md are symlinks to astrid/packs/_core/skill/SKILL.md — edit the target. Stop-hook preamble in lifecycle.py:_AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE + PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE must be updated to re-inject the new status-first instruction.
- Branch must be `reshape/sprint-1` off `reshape/sprint-0`.
- Type hints everywhere; Pathlib over os.path; no comments unless the WHY is non-obvious.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Build bottom-up so each layer is independently testable before integrating into the CLI:

1. **Branch + scaffold.** `git checkout -b reshape/sprint-1 reshape/sprint-0`. Create `astrid/core/session/` package with empty modules: paths.py, model.py, binding.py, lease.py, identity.py, config.py, discovery.py.

2. **Lease + locked-append (the apex).** First, define `class StaleTailError(EventLogError)` and `class StaleEpochError(EventLogError)` in events.py. Then write `append_event_locked(run_dir: Path, event: dict, *, expected_writer_epoch: int, expected_prev_hash: str) -> dict` that:
   - opens `run_dir / 'events.jsonl'` in append mode
   - `fcntl.flock(fh.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)` (blocking)
   - reads the current tail line; parses its hash; if `last_hash != expected_prev_hash` raise StaleTailError including both
   - reads `run_dir / 'lease.json'`'s writer_epoch; if `current != expected_writer_epoch` raise StaleEpochError
   - computes new hash from `last_hash`, writes the JSON line + newline, flushes, `os.fsync`, releases lock
   - returns the stored dict
   Keep the existing `append_event` as a thin wrapper that reads epoch + prev_hash internally — that lets legacy single-writer paths keep working without the CAS, but they still get serialized by flock so they never interleave.
   Unit-test in `tests/task/test_events_locked.py` (happy path, stale tail, stale epoch, succeeds after epoch bump).
   Then run the flock-spike pattern but invoke the new helper from two subprocesses and assert exactly-one-winner.

3. **Session model + paths.** In `session/model.py` define `@dataclass(frozen=True) class Session(id, project, timeline, run_id, agent_id, attached_at, last_used_at, role)` with `from_path/to_path` JSON helpers. In `session/paths.py` mirror `core/project/paths.py` patterns: env-var override `ASTRID_HOME` (default `~/.astrid`), helpers for `sessions_dir()`, `identity_path()`, `user_config_path()`, `workspace_config_path()`. ULID: vendor a ~20-line generator (or pull `python-ulid`) — record the choice in a one-line comment. Round-trip tests for all readers/writers.

4. **Lease.** `session/lease.py`: `read_lease(run_dir)`, `write_lease_init(run_dir, session_id, writer_epoch=0)`, `bump_epoch_and_swap_session(run_dir, new_session_id) -> int` which acquires the SAME flock on events.jsonl, increments writer_epoch, swaps attached_session.json, appends a takeover event, releases. Reuse `append_event_locked` so the takeover event itself is properly chained.

5. **Binding.** `session/binding.py::resolve_current() -> Session | None` reads `ASTRID_SESSION_ID` env, looks up the session file. `is_writer_for(session, run_dir) -> bool` compares lease.attached_session_id.

6. **Identity + config + discovery.** Identity reader/writer + first-run bootstrap (prompts for agent_id slug). Config reader merges `~/.astrid/config.json` + `.astrid/config.json` (workspace wins). Discovery enumerates projects under `resolve_projects_root()`.

7. **Verb implementations.** Add a new module `astrid/core/session/cli.py` with `cmd_attach`, `cmd_sessions_ls`, `cmd_sessions_detach`, `cmd_sessions_takeover`. Rewrite `cmd_status` in lifecycle.py (or move it) to be session-driven and print the full breadcrumb. cmd_status with no identity → bootstrap; with identity but no session → list projects + attach hint; bound → breadcrumb.

8. **CLI gate.** In `astrid/pipeline.py:main`, after the existing nudge and before `_dispatch`, add a gate: if first token is NOT in the allowlist `{attach, sessions, status, projects, init, -h, --help}` (and projects' sub-verb must be ls/create when unbound), require ASTRID_SESSION_ID to resolve a valid session, else print the error + recovery. Wire `attach`, `sessions` dispatch entries. Remove the `thread` dispatch entry.

9. **Migrate callers off active_run.py.** Sweep the 4+2 sites in lifecycle.py, gate.py:519, inbox.py:216, hook.py:40/66, orchestrate/test_runner.py:113. Replace with: resolve session → load run_dir → use lease for CAS. Route every `append_event(events_path, ev)` call through `append_event_locked(run_dir, ev, expected_writer_epoch=epoch, expected_prev_hash=prev)` where `epoch` is read at the start of the verb and `prev_hash` is read just before append. On StaleEpochError/StaleTailError, the verb exits non-zero with a clean message — the agent retries.

10. **Threads removal.** Delete `astrid/threads/`, the pipeline.py:123-126 dispatch entry, the 13 test_threads_*.py files. Update SKILL.md (the symlink target) to remove the line-60 instruction and replace with the new attach/status workflow. Run the SKILL inspect test to see what assumes threads — likely test_threads_docs_skill_inspect.py is deleted along with the rest.

11. **project.json sentinel.** Add `default_timeline_id` as optional in `build_project`. Update `validate_project` to preserve it (it already preserves unknown fields, but make it explicit + typed).

12. **Migration scripts under `scripts/migrations/sprint-1/`:**
    - `migrate_active_run_to_sessions.py` — for each `<project>/active_run.json`, materialize one session binding (using the project root's name as slug, ULID-generated session id) and delete the file.
    - `migrate_threads_to_sessions.py` — read `.astrid/threads.json` (and any per-project thread state), fold any persistent fields (e.g., variant selections) into session bindings or, if irrelevant after threads die, log + skip. Confirm with the user that no run-history field is being dropped.
    - `migrate_set_default_timeline_sentinel.py` — for each `project.json`, ensure `default_timeline_id` key exists with `null`.
    All three: `#!/usr/bin/env python3`, executable, `--dry-run` default, `--apply` flag, stderr per-action log, temp-file audit log per project.

13. **SKILL.md / AGENTS.md rewrite.** Step 1: `astrid status` always. Document `attach`, `next`, `ack`, `sessions ls/detach/takeover`. Update PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE and `_AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE` in lifecycle.py so the stop-hook re-injection teaches the new model.

14. **Tests + harness validation.** Add the listed test files. Run the two-tab harness 100 rounds on a real mutating verb (pick `astrid ack <step> --decision approve` because it has the cleanest single-event-append surface, or even a thin `astrid sessions takeover` test that exercises lease swap). Confirm exactly-one-winner. Then run the takeover-during-append test under multiple seeds.

15. **Regression sweep.** Run all of `pytest tests/` (excluding deleted thread tests). Fix any failure that turns out to be a real escape rather than test rot. Confirm Sprint 0 spike + harness tests still green. Run any hype-related smoke test under the new model and confirm functional non-regression (no byte-equivalence required per regression-workload.md).

16. **Stop-line checks before merge.**
    - Two-tab harness: 100 rounds, exactly one winner per round, no interleaving in events.jsonl.
    - Tab restart + ASTRID_SESSION_ID re-export restores binding.
    - active_run migration: dry-run shows expected actions; --apply produces session bindings + deletes file; events.jsonl unchanged; no run-history loss.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
# Sprint 1 — Sessions and the binding contract

This is Sprint 1 of the Astrid reshape (full plan: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/idea.md` — Sprint 1 section is canonical for scope; this brief expands it for execution).

Working directory: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid`. Branch: create `reshape/sprint-1` off `reshape/sprint-0` and work there.

## Goal

End multi-tab races. Make "what am I bound to" a first-class, queryable thing. Ship `astrid status` as the agent's mandated anchor verb. Lay down the lease + locked-append contract every later sprint depends on.

**No timelines yet (Sprint 2). No new step model yet (Sprint 3).** Sprint 1 is purely the session/binding/safety layer.

## Foundational invariant (the apex contract)

The single most important deliverable is the **locked event-append path**, because every later sprint runs on top of it. The contract is:

> Every event appended to `runs/<ulid>/events.jsonl` does this **atomically inside one critical section** held by `flock(events.jsonl, LOCK_EX)`:
> 1. Re-read the file's last line, parse its hash, compare against the writer's read-time hash. If they differ → reject with a stale-tail error.
> 2. Re-read `runs/<ulid>/lease.json`'s `writer_epoch`. Compare against the writer's read-time epoch. If they differ → reject with a stale-epoch error.
> 3. Compute the new event's previous-hash = the last-line hash.
> 4. Append the new event line.
> 5. fsync (or equivalent durability), then release the lock.

A stale writer that passed its initial epoch check but lost the race to a takeover **gets rejected at append time, not silently committed**. The `flock` + last-hash CAS + epoch CAS are the actual fence; the takeover *event* is just observability.

This replaces the current `append_event` in `astrid/core/task/events.py`, which does read/verify/append **without a file lock** (the bug we're fixing).

The Sprint 0 flock-on-APFS spike confirmed `flock` is reliable for this workload. Use the worked test pattern from `tests/spikes/test_flock_apfs.py` as the integration-test starting point.

## Decisions (locked; do not relitigate)

- **Session id = ULID.** Stored in `~/.astrid/sessions/<ulid>.json` with `{session_id, project, timeline, run_id, agent_id, attached_at, last_used_at, role}`. `role` ∈ `writer | reader`.
- **`ASTRID_SESSION_ID` env var binds the tab.** Subprocesses inherit through `fork`/`exec`. Sprint 0 verified all existing subprocess paths preserve env via `{**os.environ, ...}`.
- **Stable agent identity in `~/.astrid/identity.json`** with `agent_id: <slug>` (e.g. `claude-1`, `codex-research`). First-run bootstrap prompts user to set one. Sessions inherit it; per-tab override via `astrid attach --as agent:<id>`.
- **Per-user default project in `~/.astrid/config.json`; per-workspace override in `.astrid/config.json`.** Neither auto-attaches — both feed the suggestion shown by `status` when unbound.
- **Per-project default timeline pointer recorded in `project.json`** (Sprint 1 writes the field; Sprint 2 wires it into `astrid attach`). Use a sentinel value in S1's migration; Sprint 2 backfills.
- **Read-only attach when a run is held by another session.** Second attacher gets `role: reader`. Takeover is the explicit verb `astrid sessions takeover <session_id>`.
- **Run lease epoch (`writer_epoch: int`).** Stored in `runs/<ulid>/lease.json` alongside `attached_session.json` (the current writer's session id). Every mutating verb routes through the locked-append helper above.
- **Takeover atomically increments `writer_epoch`** in the same locked write that swaps `attached_session.json`. Both old session and new session see a `takeover` event in `events.jsonl` with `{prev_session, new_session, prev_epoch, new_epoch, at}`.
- **Stuck-attachment recovery via `status`.** A fresh tab's `status` proactively flags suspected-dead sessions (criterion: no event written in N seconds AND session-file mtime older than M seconds — pick honest defaults like 60s/300s) and offers takeover with a "may still be live elsewhere — confirm" warning. Manual `astrid sessions detach <id>` is the unconditional escape hatch.
- **Threads die.** `thread show @active` and friends are removed. Any persistent thread state migrates into session bindings in a one-shot pass.
- **Inbox primitive standardized.** Every run has `runs/<ulid>/inbox/`. Sessions and external systems write JSON files; `astrid next` consumes them. This is the substrate that makes async work possible from day one.
- **First-run bootstrap is part of this sprint, not assumed.** Fresh tab post-migration must not produce a string of errors. When `~/.astrid/identity.json` is missing OR no default project is set, `astrid` prompts through identity + project discovery + default selection explicitly. `astrid status` when unbound + no default lists discoverable projects with the `attach` command spelled out (not just "no session").
- **Migration sets a per-project default-timeline sentinel** even though Sprint 2 wires the actual timeline container. Otherwise Sprint 2 has to backfill across the entire project tree.
- **CLI gate.** Every verb except `attach`, `status`, `projects ls`, `projects create`, `sessions ls`, `sessions takeover`, `init` (first-run bootstrap), and `--help` errors out with a clear "no session bound" message that suggests `astrid attach <project>`.

## Deliverables

### New / rewritten files

- **`astrid/core/session/`** new package:
  - `paths.py` — helpers for `~/.astrid/sessions/<ulid>.json`, `~/.astrid/identity.json`, `~/.astrid/config.json`, `.astrid/config.json` (workspace).
  - `model.py` — `Session` dataclass (id, project, timeline, run_id, agent_id, attached_at, last_used_at, role) with json read/write.
  - `binding.py` — resolve current session via `ASTRID_SESSION_ID` env var; readers and writers; "is this session a writer for this run" checks.
  - `lease.py` — `runs/<ulid>/lease.json` reader/writer; `attached_session.json` reader/writer.
  - `identity.py` — `~/.astrid/identity.json` reader/writer + first-run bootstrap.
  - `config.py` — config file reader (user + workspace) + default project/timeline resolution.
  - `discovery.py` — list discoverable projects under the projects root for the bootstrap UX.

- **`astrid/core/task/events.py`** rewrite the append path:
  - New `append_event_locked(run_dir, event_dict, *, expected_writer_epoch, expected_prev_hash) -> EventRecord` that does flock + last-hash CAS + epoch CAS atomically. Raises `StaleTailError` / `StaleEpochError` on conflict.
  - Old `append_event` becomes a thin wrapper over the locked path that reads epoch + last-hash internally; deprecate it for callers that need the CAS guarantees.
  - All existing call sites that mutate the event log MUST route through the new locked helper.

- **`astrid/core/task/active_run.py`** delete or stub:
  - The `<project>/active_run.json` file is the multi-tab race we're fixing. Migrate any existing files into per-tab session bindings (one-shot at `astrid attach`), then remove the read/write helpers. Leave a one-line stub explaining what replaced it.

- **Threads subsystem removal:**
  - Find every `thread show @active`-style verb and any `astrid/core/thread/` (or wherever threads live). Migrate persistent thread state into session bindings (best-effort one-shot migration script). Then remove the subsystem.

### New / rewritten verbs

Use the existing CLI module (`astrid/cli/` or wherever verbs are registered — discover by reading current code).

- `astrid attach <project> [--timeline <slug>] [--session <id>] [--as agent:<id>]` — bind the current tab to a project. Creates a new session (writes `~/.astrid/sessions/<ulid>.json`), prints the `ASTRID_SESSION_ID=<ulid>` line for the user to `export`. If `--session <id>` given, resume an existing session. If a run is held by another writer, attach as reader and tell the user how to take over.
- `astrid sessions ls` — list all sessions in `~/.astrid/sessions/` with their bound project + timeline + run + last-used.
- `astrid sessions detach [<id>]` — explicit detach. With no id, detaches the current tab.
- `astrid sessions takeover <run-id|session-id>` — atomic epoch-bump + lease swap. Emits a `takeover` event into the run's `events.jsonl`. Refuses if the session being taken over wrote within the last N seconds without confirmation flag.
- `astrid status` — completely rewritten. When bound, prints the full breadcrumb: `session <id> · agent <id> · project <slug> · timeline <slug> · run <id> · current step <name> · recent events (last 5) · inbox count · role (writer/reader) · takeover hint when reader`. When unbound: lists discoverable projects, suggests defaults if set, spells out the exact `astrid attach <slug>` command. When `~/.astrid/identity.json` missing: triggers the first-run bootstrap.
- **CLI gate** on every other verb: read `ASTRID_SESSION_ID`, look up the session file, error-out with a clear hint if either is missing.

### Migration scripts

Place under `scripts/migrations/sprint-1/`:

- `migrate_active_run_to_sessions.py` — for each project with an `active_run.json`, materialize a session binding for "the calling tab" (best-effort: one session per project), then delete the file. Idempotent. Dry-run by default; `--apply` flag to commit.
- `migrate_threads_to_sessions.py` — same shape for thread state, if it exists in the codebase.
- `migrate_set_default_timeline_sentinel.py` — for each project, write a sentinel `default_timeline_id: null` field into `project.json` so Sprint 2 doesn't have to backfill.

Each script:
- Logs every action to stderr.
- Supports `--dry-run` (default) and `--apply`.
- Writes a per-project audit log to a temp file.

### SKILL.md / AGENTS.md rewrite

Both files are stale (predate the task framework, don't teach `astrid status` / `next` / `ack`). Rewrite around the new mandated workflow:

1. **First instruction**: "Run `astrid status` before any other verb. If unbound, ask the user which project."
2. Document `attach`, `status`, `next`, `ack`, `sessions ls/detach/takeover`.
3. Stop-hook preamble (if Astrid has one — discover by reading current SKILL.md) is updated to re-inject the status-first instruction when context decays.

### Tests

- **Locked-append unit tests** (`tests/task/test_events_locked.py`): single-process correctness — append, append-after-stale-tail rejected, append-after-stale-epoch rejected, append after epoch bump succeeds.
- **Two-tab integration tests** (use the existing `tests/concurrency/two_tab_harness.py` from Sprint 0): two writers race the same run; exactly one succeeds, the other gets stale-tail or stale-epoch. Run with `--count 100` to surface races.
- **Takeover atomicity test**: takeover-during-append must either (a) the append wins and takeover sees stale tail and retries, or (b) the takeover wins and the appender sees stale epoch and rejects. Never both succeed; never silent loss.
- **Session attach/detach/resume tests** (unit + small integration).
- **Migration script tests**: each script has a smoke test with a tiny fixture project tree.
- **First-run bootstrap test**: simulate missing `~/.astrid/identity.json` → bootstrap prompt path runs end-to-end (mock the prompt input).
- **CLI gate test**: every gated verb errors clearly when `ASTRID_SESSION_ID` is unset.

## Stop-lines (halt and rethink, do not push through)

1. **If the locked-append path can't be proven correct under contention** (the two-tab harness shows interleaved writes or silent epoch-violations under any seed), halt. The whole reshape is built on this guarantee.
2. **If session resume across tab restart is broken** (closing a tab + reopening + `ASTRID_SESSION_ID` re-export doesn't restore the binding), halt. The "sessions are resumable, never auto-expire" decision is load-bearing.
3. **If migration of `active_run.json` causes ANY data loss** (any project's run-history becomes unreadable), halt and restore from the Sprint 0 snapshot.

## Out of scope (Sprint 2 / 3 territory — do NOT touch)

- Timeline as container (Sprint 2). The `default_timeline_id` field is a sentinel only; do not implement the timeline schema.
- Step model rewrite (Sprint 3). `plan.py` / `inbox.py` / step adapter logic stays as-is; only the event-append path changes.
- Plan mutation verbs (Sprint 3).
- Any RunPod / vibecomfy work.
- Hype port.
- Auditing verbs (`astrid run show / artifacts / trace`) — those are Sprint 5a.

## Acceptance criteria

- `git branch --show-current` returns `reshape/sprint-1`.
- All Sprint 0 tests still pass (regression).
- `pytest tests/task/test_events_locked.py tests/concurrency/ tests/session/ -v` all green.
- Two-tab harness: race the same `astrid attach` + locked-append-via-some-verb 100 times; exactly one writer succeeds per race, the other gets a clean stale-tail or stale-epoch error.
- Fresh tab + no identity file → `astrid status` triggers first-run bootstrap, completes successfully, leaves `~/.astrid/identity.json` written.
- Fresh tab + identity but no session bound → `astrid status` lists discoverable projects + spells out `astrid attach <slug>`.
- Fresh tab + session bound → `astrid status` prints the full breadcrumb.
- `astrid sessions takeover` of a stale-looking session works; the old session's next mutating verb gets rejected with stale-epoch.
- All migration scripts run cleanly against the Sprint 0 baseline inventory (refer to `docs/reshape/inventory-baseline-20260511.csv`).
- The Sprint 0 pinned regression workload (`docs/reshape/regression-workload.md`) re-runs cleanly. **Note:** that doc reports no past hype runs exist on this machine, so the regression gate is "no functional regression" rather than "byte-equivalent output." Run any existing hype-related smoke tests and confirm green.
- SKILL.md / AGENTS.md updated; a fresh-context agent reading them can complete one full task end-to-end without asking a clarifying question.

## Useful repo references

- `astrid/core/task/events.py` — the unsafe `append_event` to replace.
- `astrid/core/task/active_run.py` — the file to migrate away from.
- `astrid/core/project/paths.py` — project / run path helpers; reuse the patterns.
- `tests/spikes/test_flock_apfs.py` — Sprint 0's flock spike; the worked pattern for the locked-append integration tests.
- `tests/concurrency/two_tab_harness.py` — Sprint 0's race harness.
- `docs/reshape/spike-env-inheritance.md` — confirms `ASTRID_SESSION_ID` survives every subprocess path.
- `docs/reshape/spike-flock-apfs.md` — confirms `flock` is reliable for this workload.
- `idea.md` § Foundation > Load-bearing decisions — apex spec.
- `idea.md` § Sprint 1 — the canonical sprint definition this brief expands.

## Style notes

- Type hints everywhere. Pathlib over os.path.
- New tests follow existing test naming + structure conventions in the repo.
- Migration scripts: shebang, `chmod +x`, `--dry-run` is the default.
- No silent fallbacks in the lease/append path — every CAS failure raises a typed exception with the conflicting values in the message.

# Implementation Plan: Astrid Sprint 1 — Sessions & the Binding Contract (v3)

## Overview

Sprint 1 of the Astrid reshape on branch `reshape/sprint-1` off `reshape/sprint-0`. The apex deliverable is a **flock-protected, last-hash + writer-epoch CAS event-append helper** that replaces the unsafe `append_event` at `astrid/core/task/events.py:25`. Around it: per-tab ULID sessions in `~/.astrid/sessions/`, a run-side `lease.json` with `writer_epoch` **and `plan_hash`** (the latter migrated out of active_run.json into the lease — see FLAG-010 fix), a project-level `current_run.json` pointer, an `astrid attach / sessions / status` CLI surface gated on every other verb, SKILL.md rewrite mandating `astrid status` step 1, and a `default_timeline_id: null` sentinel into `project.json`.

**Settled decisions (do not relitigate)** — from v2 critique:
- `astrid/threads/` STAYS as an internal library (18 production importers). Only the user-facing `astrid thread` CLI verb is removed. Full retirement in a later sprint.
- `<project>/current_run.json` (minimal `{run_id}` pointer) replaces active_run.json's discovery role.
- Existing `astrid/threads/ids.py:generate_ulid` is re-exported, not re-implemented.
- Verb functions accept `session: Session` argument; tests bypass entrypoint gate by design via shared `attached_session` fixture; CLI gate at pipeline.main() only.
- CLI allowlist is brief-canonical + `sessions detach`. `doctor`/`setup` require a session.
- Stop-hook: session-bound wins; cwd-walk fallback only when unbound; projects-root scan dropped.
- Historical `runs/<id>/AGENT.md` files left alone.
- `astrid author test --project <slug>` bypasses session gate as developer/CI path.

**v3 design fixes (the five real correctness gaps from critique_v2):**

1. **plan_hash lives in `lease.json`, not in `current_run.json`** (FLAG-010, correctness-1). The lease file is opened under the same `flock` the append helper uses, so the CAS is free. v2's "parse `run_started` from tail" was broken because `run_started` is the FIRST event and tail-seek can't find it. Schema: `lease.json = {"writer_epoch": int, "attached_session_id": str | None, "plan_hash": str}`. `cmd_start` writes it; `gate_command` reads it.
2. **`GateDecision` carries `writer_epoch` + `run_dir`** so the four post-dispatch record_* helpers (`record_dispatch_complete` at gate.py:1099, `record_step_attested` at 1186, `record_nested_entered` at 1201, `record_nested_exited` at 1211) can each open their own short WriterContext using the threaded values (FLAG-011, correctness-2, callers-3, criteria_quality). pipeline.py:59's `finally:` block constructs the WriterContext from the decision.
3. **WriterContext auto-rebinds stale `session.run_id`** on entry (correctness-3). Before reading the lease, WriterContext re-reads `current_run.json`; if it differs from `session.run_id`, the in-memory session is updated and the on-disk session file rewritten. Tab A attaches (run_id=None) → Tab B starts → Tab A's next mutating verb auto-rebinds without erroring. Matches the "sessions are resumable" decision.
4. **Role enum gains `orphan-pending`** (correctness-4). cmd_attach to a project with an unclaimed lease (attached_session_id is null but current_run.json points to a real run) sets role=`orphan-pending`, prints "no writer attached — run `astrid sessions takeover <run-id>` to claim." Role=`writer` only after lease is claimed.
5. **Inbox-abort preserves full-clear semantics** (correctness-5). `inbox.py:216` calls BOTH `clear_current_run(slug)` AND `release_writer_lease(run_dir)` on decision="abort" entries (matches existing `clear_active_run` full-clear behavior); decision="approve" path is unchanged.

**v3 design refinements (smaller):**
- **Tail-seek chunked follow-back** (correctness-7): if no newline in last 4 KB, double the window (8 KB, 16 KB, … up to file size). No 64 KB cap. Single-line files of any size are handled.
- **`verify_chain` migrated to tail-only verification at gate.py:533** (correctness-6): full-chain verify is O(n²) over a run lifecycle. Replace with: under the flock, read tail line, verify `expected_prev_hash == last_event.hash` AND `_event_hash(last_event.prev_hash, last_event) == last_event.hash` (verify just the last link). Full-chain verify becomes an explicit audit verb.
- **`cmd_next` reorders preamble-after-validate** (all_locations-3): session check runs BEFORE `PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE` is printed; unbound `astrid next` prints "no session bound" without spamming preamble.
- **SKILL.md `--thread @active` clarification** (scope, all_locations-1, issue_hints-2): line 60 (`astrid thread show @active`) is removed (CLI verb is gone). Line 62 documents iteration_video's `--thread @active` argument — this is a pack-internal concept meaning "current variant lineage," not the deleted CLI verb. Add a one-line clarification in SKILL.md: "The pack-level `--thread` argument is unrelated to the removed `astrid thread` CLI; it identifies a variant lineage within a pack." `iteration_video/run.py` and `iteration/prepare/run.py` stay unchanged.
- **callers-2 (nested staleness)** is correct behavior, not a bug: if takeover lands between outer WriterContext entry and `consume_inbox_entry → gate_command` re-entry, the inbox's append raises `StaleEpochError` → cmd_next exits non-zero → agent re-runs → fresh session resolution. Document this in the WriterContext contract: "epoch threading is not a freshness guarantee; it's a fence. If a takeover happens mid-verb, the verb fails clean and retries."

**Confirmed touch-points:**
- 20 `append_event(` call sites (gate.py × 13, including the 4 post-dispatch sites that need their own WriterContext; lifecycle.py × 2; inbox.py × 2; lifecycle_ack.py × 2).
- 7 `read_active_run` sites + 1 write + 2 clears.
- 18 test files use active_run fixtures directly (enumerated in Step 13).
- `consume_inbox_entry` at `inbox.py:259` calls `gate_command` — threads outer `writer_epoch` via kwarg; stale epoch surfaces as legitimate CAS failure.

## Phase 0: Branch & Scaffold

### Step 1: Branch and scaffold session package (`astrid/core/session/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `git checkout -b reshape/sprint-1 reshape/sprint-0`.
2. Create `astrid/core/session/` with `__init__.py`: `paths.py`, `model.py`, `binding.py`, `lease.py`, `identity.py`, `config.py`, `discovery.py`, `cli.py`, `writer.py`, `constants.py`, `ulid.py`.
3. Create `scripts/migrations/sprint-1/`.

## Phase 1: The Apex — Locked Event Append

### Step 2: Typed CAS exceptions + locked helper + lease schema (`astrid/core/task/events.py`, `astrid/core/session/lease.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `StaleTailError(EventLogError)` and `StaleEpochError(EventLogError)` with `expected`/`actual` attributes.
2. **Implement** `append_event_locked(run_dir, event, *, expected_writer_epoch, expected_prev_hash)`:
   - Resolve `events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"`, `lease_path = run_dir / "lease.json"`. `mkdir parents=True, exist_ok=True`.
   - Open events_path `"a+b"`. `fcntl.flock(fh.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)`.
   - **Tail-seek with chunked follow-back** (correctness-7):
     ```python
     window = 4096
     while True:
         pos = max(0, size - window)
         fh.seek(pos); chunk = fh.read(size - pos)
         nl_index = chunk.rfind(b"\n", 0, -1 if chunk.endswith(b"\n") else len(chunk))
         if nl_index >= 0 or pos == 0: break
         window *= 2
     # parse last non-empty line; tail_hash = its hash or ZERO_HASH if empty
     ```
   - CAS tail_hash vs `expected_prev_hash` → `StaleTailError`.
   - **Tail-only chain verify** (correctness-6): recompute `_event_hash(parsed_tail.prev_hash, parsed_tail)` and compare with `parsed_tail.hash`; on mismatch raise `EventLogError("event log corrupt at tail")`. This replaces verify_chain on the hot path AND for gate.py:533.
   - Read `lease.json`; extract `writer_epoch`. CAS → `StaleEpochError`.
   - Compute new hash, write line, flush, `os.fsync`. fsync parent on first-create.
3. **`lease.py`** schema is `{"writer_epoch": int, "attached_session_id": str | None, "plan_hash": str}` (FLAG-010 fix — plan_hash lives here):
   - `read_lease(run_dir) -> dict`: default `{"writer_epoch": 0, "attached_session_id": None, "plan_hash": ""}` if missing.
   - `write_lease_init(run_dir, *, session_id, plan_hash)`: atomic tmp+os.replace. Called by `cmd_start` after generating run_id.
   - `bump_epoch_and_swap_session(run_dir, *, new_session_id, prev_session_id, reason)`: under flock on events.jsonl, increment writer_epoch, swap attached_session_id (preserve plan_hash), then call internal `append_event_locked` body for the `takeover` event with `{prev_session, new_session, prev_epoch, new_epoch}`. Returns `(prev_epoch, new_epoch)`.
   - `claim_orphan_lease(run_dir, *, new_session_id)`: under flock, sets `attached_session_id=new_session_id` and bumps epoch (treats orphan claim as a takeover with prev_session=null). Used by `cmd_sessions_takeover` against an orphan.
   - `release_writer_lease(run_dir)`: under flock, sets `attached_session_id=None`. Used by `cmd_abort` (alongside `clear_current_run`) and `inbox.consume_inbox_entry` decision="abort" path (alongside `clear_current_run`).
4. **Legacy `append_event(path, event)`**: rewrite as test/migration-only wrapper that reads (epoch, tail) from disk and calls `append_event_locked` once. Top-of-function docstring: "Test/migration only. Production code MUST use `WriterContext`. Raises StaleTailError/StaleEpochError with no retry."

### Step 3: Unit tests (`tests/task/test_events_locked.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `test_append_event_locked_happy_path`, `test_append_event_rejects_stale_tail`, `test_append_event_rejects_stale_epoch`, `test_append_event_succeeds_after_epoch_bump`, `test_append_event_locked_tail_seek_handles_4kb_boundary`, `test_append_event_locked_tail_seek_handles_oversized_single_event` (writes a 256 KB event, then a tiny one, verifies tail extraction follows-back correctly).

### Step 4: Subprocess race test (`tests/concurrency/test_locked_append_two_tab.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Two subprocesses, spawn + Barrier, each loops N append calls with current tail+epoch read just-before-call. Assert total events = 2×N, no partial lines, chain ok.
2. 100 rounds — STOP-LINE.

### Step 9: WriterContext with auto-rebind (`astrid/core/session/writer.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `WriterContext`:
   ```python
   def __enter__(self):
       # AUTO-REBIND (correctness-3 fix):
       on_disk_run_id = read_current_run(self.session.project)
       if on_disk_run_id != self.session.run_id:
           self.session = replace(self.session, run_id=on_disk_run_id, last_used_at=now())
           self.session.to_json(session_path(self.session.id))  # update session file
       if self.session.run_id is None:
           raise NoRunBoundError(...)  # caller catches and prints clean error
       self.run_dir = project_dir(self.session.project) / "runs" / self.session.run_id
       lease = read_lease(self.run_dir)
       self.expected_writer_epoch = lease["writer_epoch"]
       self.plan_hash = lease["plan_hash"]  # cached for gate_command's CAS
       return self

   def append(self, event):
       tail = _read_tail_hash(self.run_dir / "events.jsonl")  # 4KB tail-seek
       return append_event_locked(self.run_dir, event,
                                  expected_writer_epoch=self.expected_writer_epoch,
                                  expected_prev_hash=tail)
   ```
2. **Helper for post-dispatch sites**: also expose `writer_context_from_decision(decision: GateDecision) -> WriterContext` — constructs a fresh context using `decision.run_dir` + `decision.writer_epoch_at_dispatch`. Used by record_dispatch_complete and the 3 phase-5 record_* helpers.
3. **Docstring contract**: "Epoch threading is a fence, not a freshness guarantee. If a takeover lands mid-verb, append raises StaleEpochError; the verb exits non-zero and the agent re-runs with a fresh session. This is by design (callers-2)."
4. Tests at `tests/session/test_writer_context.py`: happy path, stale-epoch surfacing, auto-rebind across run_id change.

## Phase 3: CLI Surface — Status, Attach, Sessions, Gate

### Step 10: Session CLI verbs (`astrid/core/session/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`cmd_attach`** (correctness-4 fix — `orphan-pending` role):
   - args: `<project>`, `--timeline`, `--session <id>`, `--as agent:<id>`. Identity bootstrap if missing.
   - If `--session`: load existing session, print export.
   - Else mint ULID. Read `<project>/current_run.json`:
     - **Absent** → `run_id=None`, `role=writer` (no run yet; cmd_start will create one).
     - **Present + lease.attached_session_id non-null + matches another session** → `role=reader`, print takeover hint.
     - **Present + lease.attached_session_id null** → `role=orphan-pending`, print "Run is unclaimed. Run `astrid sessions takeover <run-id>` to take it over."
   - Write session file, print `export ASTRID_SESSION_ID=<id>`.
2. **`cmd_sessions_ls`** — list with id/project/timeline/run/role/last_used.
3. **`cmd_sessions_detach [<id>]`** — remove session file.
4. **`cmd_sessions_takeover <run-id|session-id>`** — resolve; if target lease.attached_session_id is null, call `claim_orphan_lease(run_dir, new_session_id=current.id)`; else call `bump_epoch_and_swap_session(...)`. If target's lease mtime < `STUCK_NO_EVENT_SECONDS`, require `--force`. Print `prev_epoch → new_epoch`. Updates current session's role to `writer`.
5. **`cmd_status`** — same branching as v2. Literal breadcrumb template:
   ```
   session  <id>
   agent    <id>
   project  <slug>
   timeline <slug-or-none>
   run      <id-or-none>
   step     <name-or-none>
   role     <writer|reader|orphan-pending>
   inbox    <n>
   recent events:
     - <ts> <kind>
     - ...
   ```
6. Tests at `tests/session/test_session_attach_detach.py`, `test_session_resume.py`, `test_session_takeover.py`, `test_first_run_bootstrap.py`, `test_status_breadcrumb.py`.

**Scope:** Medium

   - Parse verb (with sub-verb for `projects`/`sessions`).
   - Allowlist unbound: `attach`, `status`, `projects ls`, `projects create`, `sessions ls`, `sessions takeover`, `sessions detach`, `init`, `-h`, `--help`. `author test --project <slug>` allowlisted only with explicit `--project`.
   - Non-allowlisted + no session → stderr `no session bound — run \`astrid attach <project>\``, exit 2.
2. **Remove** `thread` dispatch at `pipeline.py:123-126` and import at line 124.
3. **Add** dispatch entries for `attach`, `sessions`.
4. **pipeline.main()'s `finally:` block at line 59** (correctness-2 fix): captures `GateDecision` from `_dispatch`; constructs WriterContext via `writer_context_from_decision(decision)` and calls `record_dispatch_complete(decision, returncode, ...)` inside it.

## Phase 4: Migrate Callers Off active_run.json

### Step 12: Migrate production sites + post-dispatch records
**Scope:** Large
1. **Pattern** for every verb:
   ```python
   session = resolve_current_session()  # passed in by main()
   with writer_context(session) as wctx:
       # ... wctx.run_dir, wctx.expected_writer_epoch, wctx.plan_hash available
       wctx.append(event_dict)
   ```
2. **GateDecision schema change**: add fields `run_dir: Path`, `writer_epoch_at_dispatch: int`, `session_id: str`. Set inside gate_command's WriterContext entry.
3. **Enumerated sites**:
   - `lifecycle.py:154` (cmd_start guard): `read_current_run(slug) is not None` → reject. Preserves "one active run per project."
   - `lifecycle.py:201` (cmd_start): generate run_id; `write_current_run(slug, run_id)`; `write_lease_init(run_dir, session_id=session.id, plan_hash=<computed from plan>)`. Session.run_id is updated + session file rewritten. Subsequent verb invocations from this tab use the fresh run_id; other tabs auto-rebind on next mutating verb.
   - `lifecycle.py:205, 255, 269, 336, 212, 296` (6 append sites): use WriterContext.
   - `lifecycle.py:246, 284, 418` and `lifecycle_ack.py:143` (read sites): replace with `read_current_run + read_lease`. plan_hash comes from `lease["plan_hash"]` (FLAG-010 fix).
   - **`lifecycle.py:256` (cmd_abort clear)**: `clear_current_run(slug)` + `release_writer_lease(run_dir)`. Full clear — matches existing semantic.
   - **`lifecycle.py:413-416` cmd_next preamble re-order** (all_locations-3): move session check BEFORE `print(PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE)`. If unbound → "no session bound" error, no preamble. If bound → preamble + status.
   - **`inbox.py:212` (run_aborted append for decision="abort")**: WriterContext + `wctx.append(make_run_aborted_event(...))`.
   - **`inbox.py:216` (decision="abort" full clear)** (correctness-5 fix): `clear_current_run(slug)` AND `release_writer_lease(run_dir)`. Matches existing `clear_active_run` behavior; not a downgrade to lease-only.
   - **`inbox.py:259` (consume_inbox_entry → gate_command)** (callers-2): outer WriterContext captures `expected_writer_epoch`; pass via `gate_command(..., writer_epoch=outer_epoch)`. If takeover lands between → StaleEpochError on first append → cmd_next exits non-zero; agent re-runs. By design, not a bug.
   - **`inbox.py:296` (cursor_rewind append)**: WriterContext.
   - **`gate.py:519` (plan_hash CAS)** (FLAG-010): `read_current_run(slug)` for run_id; `read_lease(run_dir)["plan_hash"]` for CAS against the verb's `plan_hash` arg. Single dict read inside the WriterContext.
   - **`gate.py:533` (verify_chain)** (correctness-6): replace with tail-only verification (already done inside `append_event_locked` via Step 2's tail-only chain check; gate.py drops the call).
   - **`gate.py:551, 832, 848, 942, 963` (5 sites inside gate_command body)**: wrap gate_command's body in WriterContext at entry; replace each `append_event(events_path, ev)` with `wctx.append(ev)`. Decision returned includes `wctx.expected_writer_epoch` and `wctx.run_dir`.
   - **`gate.py:1105 (record_dispatch_complete), :1186 (record_step_attested), :1201 (record_nested_entered), :1211 (record_nested_exited)`** (FLAG-011, correctness-2): each helper now takes `decision: GateDecision` as a parameter and opens its own WriterContext via `writer_context_from_decision(decision)`. pipeline.py:59 wraps record_dispatch_complete in this context. The 3 phase-5-reserved helpers do the same pattern when lifecycle invokes them.
   - **`gate.py:1136, 1146, 1155, 1164` (inline-checks inside _dispatch_*)**: these are inside gate_command's WriterContext already (confirmed by critique: `_run_inline_checks` is called inside `_dispatch_code/_dispatch_attested`). Use `wctx.append`.
   - **`gate.py:1195, 1205, 1215`** (3 sites): determine each call site's caller — if inside gate_command body, use `wctx`; if in a record_* helper, use `writer_context_from_decision`.
   - **`hook.py:_walk_cwd_ancestors` + `_scan_projects_root`**: session-bound wins; cwd-walk fallback only if unbound; projects-root scan dropped.
   - **`orchestrate/test_runner.py:113`**: `read_active_run` → `read_current_run`. `astrid author test --project` bypasses session gate.
4. **Delete** `astrid/core/task/active_run.py`. Remove re-exports from `astrid/core/task/__init__.py` lines 3, 82, 103, 120.
5. **Sweep**: `grep -r 'active_run' astrid/ tests/` — only docs, migration script, current_run substring matches remain.

### Step 20: Manual stop-line checks
**Scope:** Small
1. Two-tab harness 100 rounds — exactly one winner.
2. Tab restart + re-export `ASTRID_SESSION_ID` → status restores binding.
3. **Auto-rebind**: Tab A attaches (run_id=None); Tab B starts run; Tab A's `astrid next` rebinds to the run without error.
4. **Inbox-abort**: `decision=abort` entry → after consume, current_run.json cleared + lease released (matches existing semantic).
5. Active-run migration: dry-run is no-op; --apply writes lease.json with migrated plan_hash, current_run.json with run_id, deletes active_run.json, events.jsonl byte-identical.
6. First-run bootstrap: rm identity.json → status runs bootstrap.
7. Hype smoke per `docs/reshape/regression-workload.md` — threads/ stays so orchestrator/runner.py path unchanged.
8. **Takeover-during-inbox-consume**: takeover lands while consume_inbox_entry is mid-flight → consume's gate_command append raises StaleEpochError → cmd_next exits non-zero → re-run rebinds and re-consumes. Confirms callers-2 contract.

## Execution Order
1. Locked-append + exceptions + lease schema with plan_hash (Phase 1) before anything else.
2. Race tests green at 100 rounds — STOP-LINE before Phase 2.
3. Session model + lease + current_run pointer (Phase 2 / Step 7) before CLI verbs.
4. WriterContext with auto-rebind + `writer_context_from_decision` (Step 9) before migrating callers.
5. CLI verbs + gate (Phase 3) — gate flips on after verbs exist.
6. Migrate 20 append sites + 7 read sites + 18 test files (Phase 4) — including the 4 post-dispatch record_* helpers via GateDecision threading.
7. Retire `astrid thread` CLI verb (Phase 5).
8. Schema sentinel + migration scripts (Phases 6–7) in parallel.
9. Docs sweep (Phase 8) last.
10. Validation (Phase 9).

## Validation Order
1. `test_events_locked.py` — apex contract logic (incl. oversized-event tail-seek).
2. `test_locked_append_two_tab.py` — apex under contention. STOP-LINE.
3. `tests/session/` — model/binding/identity/config/lease/writer (incl. auto-rebind).
4. `tests/test_task_kernel_*.py` + `tests/test_lifecycle_*.py` after migration. STOP-LINE.
5. `test_takeover_atomicity.py` + takeover-during-inbox-consume — takeover fence.
6. `scripts/migrations/sprint-1/tests/` — migration scripts (incl. plan_hash → lease migration).
7. Full `pytest tests/`.
8. Manual stop-line checks (Step 20).

      "reason": "Same as FLAG-010: plan_hash lives in lease.json; gate_command's CAS reads it via the same lease read WriterContext already does."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-011",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "GateDecision gains writer_epoch_at_dispatch + run_dir + session_id; the four post-dispatch record_* helpers (record_dispatch_complete, record_step_attested, record_nested_entered, record_nested_exited) each open their own WriterContext via writer_context_from_decision(decision). pipeline.py:59's finally block constructs the context."
    },

      "id": "callers-3",

    },
    {
      "id": "criteria_quality",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as callers-3: the strict 'every site uses WriterContext' criterion now matches a strategy that actually reaches all 20 sites including the out-of-body ones."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-3",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "WriterContext.__enter__ re-reads current_run.json before reading the lease. If on-disk run_id differs from session.run_id, the in-memory session is updated via dataclasses.replace and the session file is rewritten. Tab A's run_id=None auto-rebinds to Tab B's started run."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-4",

      "reason": "Role enum becomes Literal['writer', 'reader', 'orphan-pending']. cmd_attach to a project with current_run.json present but lease.attached_session_id=null sets role='orphan-pending' and prints the takeover hint. cmd_sessions_takeover handles the orphan case via claim_orphan_lease()."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-5",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "inbox.py:216 decision='abort' path now calls BOTH clear_current_run(slug) AND release_writer_lease(run_dir), matching the existing clear_active_run full-clear semantic. No behavior regression."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-6",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "verify_chain at gate.py:533 replaced with tail-only verification (recompute _event_hash on parsed tail line, compare to stored hash) done inside append_event_locked under the same flock. Full-chain verify becomes an explicit audit verb only."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-7",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Tail-seek now uses chunked follow-back: starts at 4KB, doubles to 8KB / 16KB / ... up to file size, until a newline is found. No 64KB cap. Handles single events of arbitrary size including >64KB feedback payloads."
    },
    {
      "id": "callers-1",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Verb-takes-Session signature change confirmed; documented in astrid/core/session/__init__.py. Bundled with the 18-test-file migration in Step 13. High churn accepted as the consequence of moving to session-first."
    },
    {
      "id": "callers-2",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Documented in WriterContext contract: epoch threading is a fence, not a freshness guarantee. If takeover lands mid-verb, append raises StaleEpochError, verb exits non-zero, agent re-runs. By design, not a bug. Step 20 manual check #8 verifies this."
    },

    "Confirm: `plan_hash` storage location moves into `lease.json` (the same file the writer-epoch fence reads under flock) rather than keeping it in `current_run.json`. This is the cleanest fix for the broken 'parse run_started from tail' design in v2.",
    "Confirm: the `orphan-pending` role correctly captures the 'current_run.json present but lease unclaimed' state, and cmd_sessions_takeover is the only path that claims an orphan (via the new `claim_orphan_lease` lease helper that bumps the epoch under flock).",
    "Confirm: WriterContext auto-rebind (re-reading current_run.json on entry and updating session.run_id silently) is the right model for the Tab-A-attached-while-Tab-B-started case \u2014 rather than erroring with 'session stale, reattach'.",
    "Confirm: `astrid doctor` and `astrid setup` stay gated (require session). First-run troubleshooting flows through `astrid status` (triggers identity bootstrap) and `astrid init` instead."
  ],

SKILL.md line 62 (`python3 -m astrid.packs.builtin.iteration_video.run inspect <thread>`) and iteration_video/run.py still use the thread concept, so agent-facing docs cannot fully scrub `thread` references without breaking iteration_video docs.",

    "id": "issue_hints-3",
    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Brief lists `astrid sessions detach` as a deliverable verb but omits it from its own unbound allowlist. Plan rightly allowlists detach but does not flag that the brief is internally inconsistent on this point.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-1",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan-hash CAS migration is broken. Current gate.py:528 reads `active_run[\"plan_hash\"]` for CAS. Plan v2 strips plan_hash from current_run.json (only `{run_id}`) and says CAS becomes 'plan_hash arg matches the plan-hash of the current run's events.jsonl run_started event (cheap: tail-seek + parse run_started \u2192 cache it on first read in gate_command).' But `run_started` is the FIRST event in the log, not the tail \u2014 tail-seek of the last 4KB cannot find it on any non-trivial run. Either the CAS scans the whole file (defeats the perf claim) or plan_hash must be stored elsewhere (lease.json or current_run.json). This is a real correctness gap introduced by v2's design.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-2",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: record_dispatch_complete (gate.py:1099, called from pipeline.py:59 AFTER _dispatch returns) plus record_step_attested (1186), record_nested_entered (1201), record_nested_exited (1211) all call append_event OUTSIDE the gate_command body. Plan v2 step 12 says 'wrap entire gate_command body in a WriterContext at function entry' but these record_* helpers run after gate_command returns, so they fall outside the WriterContext. The 'every append site uses WriterContext' criterion cannot be met without explicitly handling these callers \u2014 either by extending GateDecision to carry the writer epoch into pipeline.main()'s record_dispatch_complete call, or by re-resolving session+epoch in each record_* helper.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-3",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Session.run_id staleness: cmd_attach sets run_id=None when current_run.json is absent. cmd_start later writes current_run.json + lease + updates the session file's run_id. But if Tab A attached (run_id=None), Tab B starts the run, Tab A's on-disk session file is NOT updated by Tab B. Tab A now has a stale session.run_id=None while current_run.json points to a real run. Plan does not specify whether Tab A's subsequent verbs auto-rebind (re-read current_run.json) or error out. This is the same multi-tab-state-divergence class of bug the sprint is fixing.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-4",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: cmd_attach 'present and unowned \u2192 role=writer, do NOT yet acquire the lease' is confusing: cmd_start would reject (current_run.json present), and only `astrid sessions takeover` can claim the orphan lease. So role=writer without claim is effectively a degraded reader \u2014 the session cannot mutate until takeover.       "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked v5's worker auth story against `/users/user_c042661f/documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` and `worker.py`. `worker_jwt.verify_user_jwt` validates signature/audience/expiry and returns the jwt subject; it does not prove project or timeline ownership. the reference worker separately calls `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)` via a service-role read of `projects.user_id` before writing. v5 says `worker_jwt.py` confirms the user owns the timeline and only tests missing ownership claims, but it does not add the separate project-ownership check, which is required when the terminal write uses service-role.",

Critique v3 complete.

**Verified resolutions (8):** FLAG-010 (plan_hash in lease.json), FLAG-011 (GateDecision threading + writer_context_from_decision), FLAG-012 (WriterContext auto-rebind), FLAG-013 (orphan-pending role), FLAG-014 (inbox-abort full clear), FLAG-015 (verify_chain dropped from gate-entry), FLAG-016 (chunked follow-back tail-seek), FLAG-018 (SKILL.md line-62 clarification).

**Two new significant correctness findings introduced by v3:**

1. **FLAG-019: Tail-only verify formula is wrong.** Plan says `_event_hash(parsed_tail.prev_hash, parsed_tail)` but events.py never stores a `prev_hash` field — only `hash`. To recompute the tail's hash you must read the second-to-last line's `hash`. Either drop tail-only verify (the CAS already does it) or extend the tail-seek to parse two lines.

2. **FLAG-020: WriterContext lacks writer-authorization check.** v3's auto-rebind captures `lease['writer_epoch']` but never verifies `lease['attached_session_id'] == session.id`. A reader/orphan-pending session can enter WriterContext, see matching epoch, and successfully append. The epoch CAS only catches takeovers; it does NOT catch unauthorized writers that have the same epoch. Add `if lease['attached_session_id'] != session.id: raise NotWriterError` at __enter__.

**Minor flags (FLAG-021..027):** lease/current_run write-ordering for safe TOCTOU, weakened mid-chain corruption detection, SD-023 preamble invariant violation in cmd_next reorder, auto-rebind side-effect on session file, migration behavior on malformed active_run.json, recurring jsonio convention debt, and verification coverage gaps (takeover retry bound, orphan-pending attach test, multi-historical-runs determinism test, two-tab fixture pattern).

All mutating verbs and inbox consumption must route through that locked path. Sprint 1 also adds `~/.astrid` identity/config/sessions plumbing, a new `astrid attach / sessions / status` CLI surface with a session gate on every other verb, deletes `<project>/active_run.json` and the `astrid threads` subsystem (migrating both into session bindings), and writes a `default_timeline_id: null` sentinel into `project.json` for Sprint 2.
